    INNER JOIN `p_previous_medical_records` AS `p8` ON `p7`.`patient_id` = `p8`.`patient_id`
    WHERE (`p7`.`patient_id` = @__patientId_0) AND (`p0`.`record_id` = `p8`.`record_id`)
    LIMIT 1), (
    SELECT `p9`.`lname`
    FROM `patientinfo` AS `p9`
    INNER JOIN `p_previous_medical_records` AS `p10` ON `p9`.`patient_id` = `p10`.`patient_id`
    WHERE (`p9`.`patient_id` = @__patientId_0) AND (`p0`.`record_id` = `p10`.`record_id`)
    LIMIT 1), COALESCE((
    SELECT `p11`.`gender`
    FROM `patientinfo` AS `p11`
    INNER JOIN `p_previous_medical_records` AS `p12` ON `p11`.`patient_id` = `p12`.`patient_id`
    WHERE (`p11`.`patient_id` = @__patientId_0) AND (`p0`.`record_id` = `p12`.`record_id`)
    LIMIT 1), '')
FROM `patientinfo` AS `p`
INNER JOIN `p_previous_medical_records` AS `p0` ON `p`.`patient_id` = `p0`.`patient_id`
WHERE `p`.`patient_id` = @__patientId_0
GROUP BY `p0`.`record_id`
LIMIT 1

    FROM `patientinfo` AS `p1`
    INNER JOIN `p_previous_medical_records` AS `p2` ON `p1`.`patient_id` = `p2`.`patient_id`

    FROM `patientinfo` AS `p3`
    INNER JOIN `p_previous_medical_records` AS `p4` ON `p3`.`patient_id` = `p4`.`patient_id`
    WHERE (`p3`.`patient_id` = @__patientId_0) AND (`p0`.`record_id` = `p4`.`record_id`)

    FROM `patientinfo` AS `p5`
    INNER JOIN `p_previous_medical_records` AS `p6` ON `p5`.`patient_id` = `p6`.`patient_id`
    WHERE (`p5`.`patient_id` = @__patientId_0) AND (`p0`.`record_id` = `p6`.`record_id`)
    LIMIT 1), (

    FROM `patientinfo` AS `p7`
    INNER JOIN `p_previous_medical_records` AS `p8` ON `p7`.`patient_id` = `p8`.`patient_id`
    WHERE (`p7`.`patient_id` = @__patientId_0) AND (`p0`.`record_id` = `p8`.`record_id`)

FROM `patientinfo` AS `p`
